Helpers `rotateLeft`, `generateMasks`, `generateBitmask`, and a `Case(addr, size, data, mask)` class.\n\nSingle test `\"Aligned Write then Read\"` iterates 56 cases covering:\n- 4-byte aligned writes at every 4-byte boundary in 32 bytes (`0x00`..`0x1c`)\n- 2-byte aligned writes at every 2-byte boundary\n- 1-byte writes at every byte location\n\nFor each case it: pokes `dbus.valid/addr/write/wdata=(data << addr*8)/wmask/size`, accepts the AXI write addr and verifies `addr=alignedAddr (addr - addr%size)`, `size=log2Ceil(c.size)`, `len=0`; accepts the write data and verifies `data=shiftedData & generateBitmask(mask)` and `strb=mask`; sends `bresp` and confirms `dbus.ready`. Then issues a read at the same address and verifies the AXI read addr `len=0, size=log2Ceil(size), addr=c.addr`, accepts a read response with `last=true`.\n\n---\n\n## 6. /app/hdl/chisel/src/coralnpu/FabricTest.scala — FULL CONTENT\n\nPackage `coralnpu`.\n\n`FabricArbiterSpec extends AnyFreeSpec with ChiselSim` (default 2 sources) tests:\n- `\"CoralNPU Read - AXI Slave Any\"` — source(0) read priority over source(1), checks `fabricBusy(1)=1`, propagates response back.\n- `\"CoralNPU Write - AXI Slave Any\"` — source(0) write priority.\n- `\"CoralNPU None - AXI Slave Read\"` — when source(0) is idle, source(1) read wins, `fabricBusy(1)=0`.\n- `\"CoralNPU None - AXI Slave Write\"` — same for write.\n- `\"Both None\"` — no transactions, all signals zero.\n- `\"3-Port Arbiter Priority\"` — uses `new FabricArbiter(p, n=3)`. Verifies port 0 > port 1 > port 2 priority and `fabricBusy` settings; broadcast response is delivered to all 3 sources.\n\n`FabricMuxSpec extends AnyFreeSpec with ChiselSim` uses memoryRegions:\n- IMEM at `0x0000` size `0x2000`\n- DMEM at `0x10000` size `0x8000`\n- Peripheral at `0x30000` size `0x2000`\n\nTests `\"Writes\"` and `\"Reads\"` route input addresses `0x10`, `0x10020`, `0x30004` to ports yielding output addresses `0x10`, `0x20`, `0x4`; verifies invalid address `0x90000` is dropped on all ports; checks `periBusy(i)` propagates to `fabricBusy`.\n\n---\n\n## 7. /app/hdl/chisel/src/coralnpu/ClockGateTest.scala — FULL CONTENT\n\nPackage `coralnpu`.\n- `ClockGateTester` Module extends `RequireAsyncReset`: instantiates `new ClockGate()` with `clk_i := clock`, `enable := io.enable`, `te := false.B`. Contains testers `ForceZeroTester`, `Zip32Tester`, `RotateVectorLeftTester`, `RotateVectorRightTester`, `ShiftVectorLeftTester`, `ShiftVectorRightTester` (all 16 lanes for vector rotates/shifts).\n\n`LibrarySpec extends AnyFreeSpec with ChiselSim`:\n- `\"ForceZero when invalid\"` — invalid input poked 9001 yields out=0.\n- `\"ForceZeroForceZero propogates when valid\"` — valid input passes through.\n- `\"Zip32 Words\"` — sz=4, a=5, b=3163 → `(3163<<32)|5`.\n- `\"Zip32 Halves\"` — sz=2, a=`(7<<16)|3`, b=`(11<<16)|5` → `(11<<48)|(7<<32)|(5<<16)|3`.\n- `\"Zip32 Bytes\"` — sz=1, packs four bytes from each.\n- `\"RotateVectorLeft\"` / `\"RotateVectorRight\"` — for shifts 0..15 verifies cyclic mapping.\n- `\"ShiftVectorLeft\"` / `\"ShiftVectorRight\"` — non-cyclic; positions that would wrap are forced to zeros.\n\n---\n\n## 10. /app/hdl/chisel/src/common/FIFOStateTest.scala — FULL CONTENT\n\nPackage `common`. `FIFOStateTestWrapper(t: UInt, n: Int, capacity: Int)` Module holds `RegInit(FIFOState.init(capacity, t))`, computes `deqState = state.dequeue(deqReady)` then `enqState = deqState.enqueue(enqData, enqValid)`; on `flush` sets state to `state.flush()`. Asserts `state.invariant()` every cycle. Outputs `count` and `peek(n)`.\n\n`FIFOStateSpec extends AnyFreeSpec with ChiselSim` uses default `(UInt(32.W), 4, 16)` for most tests:\n- `\"FIFOState Basic Test\"` — enq sequences (3,5), then 9001, then deq 1; verify `nEnqueued` and `dataOut`.\n- `\"Write n\"` — pushes 4 items in one cycle.\n- `\"Fill Buffer\"` — fills to 16 in 4 transactions of n=4.\n- `\"Fill and Empty Buffer\"` — fills then dequeues.\n- `\"Fill, Remove 4 items, and fill back to the top\"`.\n- `\"Flush Test\"` — fills then flushes; counts return to 0 after 2 cycles.\n- `\"Read and Write Buffer on Same Cycle\"`.\n- `\"Simultaneous Read Write\"` — when full, simultaneous enq=4/deq=4 keeps count at 16; oldest data peek shows next batch (100..103).\n- `\"Non-Power-of-2 Size (capacity=5)\"` — uses `FIFOStateTestWrapper(UInt(32.W), 4, 5)` to verify wrap-around correctness.\n\n---\n\n## 11. /app/hdl/chisel/src/coralnpu/scalar/MluTest.scala — FULL CONTENT\n\nPackage `coralnpu`. `MluSpec extends AnyFreeSpec with ChiselSim`, val `p = new Parameters`. after reset deassert, fetchAddr.valid=1, bits=0x20000000.\n- `\"Branch\"` — branch to `0x30000000`; after a fetch at `0x20000000`, the next fetch becomes `0x30000000` then `0x30000020`.\n- `\"FetchAligned\"` — buffers 8, next fetch advances by 0x20.\n- `\"FetchWithBranch\"` — when inst(3) is `0x0000006f` (jal), `bufferRequest.nValid=4` and next fetch is `0x2000000c`.\n- `\"Backpressure\"` — verifies fetch stalls when bufferSpaces=4.\n- `\"BackpressureStaged\"` — staged backpressure where 8 are accepted then more 8.\n\n`FetcherSpec extends AnyFreeSpec with ChiselSim` testing `Fetcher(p)`:\n- `\"Initialization\"`.\n- `\"Fetch\"` — issues `ctrl.bits=32, valid=true`; Two `generate_linker_script` invocations (`coralnpu_tcm_ld` with itcm=8/dtcm=32, and `coralnpu_tcm_highmem_ld` with itcm=1024/dtcm=1024). `filegroup all_files` includes `//toolchain/wrappers:all` and `@toolchain_coralnpu_v2//:all_files`. Two `toolchain` registrations binding them to platforms `//platforms/cpu:coralnpu_v2` × `(@platforms//os:none | //platforms/os:semihosting)`. `sh_binary gdb` driven by `//toolchain/wrappers:gdb`.\n\n### /app/toolchain/cc_toolchain_config.bzl — FULL\n\nDefines `coralnpu_v2_cc_toolchain_config` rule. Features:\n- `printf_float` (disabled by default, links with `-u _printf_float`).\n- `warnings` (enabled): `-Wall -Werror`.\n- `includes` (enabled): `-nostdinc` and `-isystem` paths into `external/toolchain_coralnpu_v2/...` for c++/15.1.0 headers and `riscv32-unknown-elf/include`.\n- Architecture set: `-march=rv32imf_zve32f_zicsr_zifencei_zbb -mabi=ilp32 -mcmodel=medany -nostdlib`.\n- Spec set (link): non-semihosting uses `--specs=nano.specs -Wl,--start-group -lstdc++ -lm -lc -lgcc -Wl,--end-group -nostartfiles`. Semihosting uses `--specs=htif_nano.specs -lsemihost -Wl,--start-group -lstdc++ -lm -lc -lgcc -Wl,--end-group`.\n- `sys_spec` enabled feature, combining architecture + spec.\n- `baseline_compilation_flags` (enabled): `-fno-rtti`, `-fno-exceptions`, `-Wl,--gc-sections`.\n- `define_ndebug` (disabled by default).\n- `opt` (`-O3 -ffunction-sections -fdata-sections`), `dbg` (`-g3 -Og`), `fastbuild` (`-g3 -O1`), each provides `compilation_mode`.\n- `strip_debug_symbols` and `generate_linker_map` (`-Wl,-Map,%{output_execpath}.map`).\n- Tool paths: clang/gcc/ld/ar/cpp/objcopy/objdump go to `wrappers/<name>`; gcov/nm/strip set to `/bin/false`.\n- `target_cpu=\"riscv32\"`, `target_libc=\"newlib\"`, `compiler=\"clang\"`, `abi_version=\"ilp32\"`.\n\n### /app/toolchain/coralnpu_tcm.ld.tpl — FULL\n\nLinker template with placeholders `@@ITCM_LENGTH@@`, `@@DTCM_ORIGIN@@`, `@@DTCM_LENGTH@@`, `@@STACK_SIZE@@`, `@@HEAP_SIZE_SPEC@@`, `@@HEAP_LOCATION@@`, `@@STACK_START_SPEC@@`. Memory map:\n- `ITCM(rx) ORIGIN=0x00000000 LENGTH=@@ITCM_LENGTH@@K`\n- `DTCM(rw) ORIGIN=@@DTCM_ORIGIN@@ LENGTH=@@DTCM_LENGTH@@K`\n- `EXTMEM(rw) ORIGIN=0x20000000 LENGTH=4096K`\n- `DDR(rw) ORIGIN=0x80000000 LENGTH=2048M`\n\nSections placed:\n- ITCM: `.text` (`._init`, `.text*`), `.init.array`, `.fini.array`, `.rodata` (incl. `.srodata*`, `.data.rel.ro*`).\n- DTCM: `.tdata`, `.tbss` (NOLOAD), `.htif`, `.data` (with `_global_pointer = . + 0x800`, `_ret`), `.bss`, `.noinit` (NOLOAD), `.stack`.\n- EXTMEM: `.extdata`, `.extbss` (NOLOAD).\n- DDR: `.ddr_data` (also `.cnidoom.wad` and `.cnidoom.weights`), `.ddr_bss` (NOLOAD).\n- `.heap` placed in `@@HEAP_LOCATION@@`.\n\n`STACK_SIZE = DEFINED(__stack_size__) ? __stack_size__ : @@STACK_SIZE@@; __stack_shift = 7; __boot_hart = 0; ENTRY(_start);`\n\n### /app/toolchain/crt/BUILD — FULL\n\n```python\npackage(default_visibility = [\"//visibility:public\"])\n\ncc_library(\n    name = \"crt\",\n    srcs = [\"crt.S\", \"coralnpu_exceptions.cc\", \"coralnpu_gloss.cc\",\n            \"coralnpu_start.S\", \"cxx_guards.cc\"],\n    copts = [\"-DSKIP_HTIF_SYMBOLS\"],\n    alwayslink = True,\n    target_compatible_with = [\"//platforms/cpu:coralnpu_v2\"],\n)\n\ncc_library(\n    name = \"crt_semihosting\",\n    srcs = [\"crt.S\", \"coralnpu_exceptions.cc\", \"coralnpu_htif_gloss.cc\",\n            \"coralnpu_start.S\"],\n    alwayslink = True,\n    target_compatible_with = [\"//platforms/cpu:coralnpu_v2\"],\n)\n```\n\n### /app/toolchain/crt/coralnpu_start.S — Behavior\n\n`_start` (in `._init`, weak): clears `minstret`/`minstreth`, reads them into a0/a1, sets `sp=__stack_end__`, `gp=_global_pointer`, zeros all GPRs (tp/t1/t2/s0/s1/a1..a7/s2..s11/t3..t6), zeros `.bss` via `crt_section_clear`, runs `__init_array_*`, sets `mtvec` to `coralnpu_exception_handler`, sets FS+VS bits in mstatus (`0x6600`), seeds `_ret` with `0x0badd00d`, calls `main(argv=0, argc=0)`, calls `__cxa_finalize`, runs reverse `__fini_array_*`, stores return value in `_ret`. If non-zero falls through to `failure: ebreak; loop: j loop`. If zero, reads `minstret`/`minstreth` and emits the custom mpause word `0x08000073`.\n\n### /app/toolchain/crt/crt.S — Behavior\n\n`.crt` section (`\"ax\", @progbits`). Provides `crt_section_clear(a0=start, a1=end)` (word-aligned check, ebreaks on misuse) and `crt_section_copy(a0=dst_start, a1=dst_end, a2=src)` (word-aligned, validates non-destructive overlap, ebreaks on error).\n\n### /app/toolchain/crt/coralnpu_gloss.cc — Behavior\n\nNewlib syscall stubs (no HTIF). `__dso_handle` self-references. `_close=-1`, `_fstat` allows only stdout/stderr (S_IFCHR), `_isatty` returns 1 with ENOTTY, `_lseek` only allows stdout/stderr, `_read=EBADF`, `_write` line-buffers up to LOG_MAX_SZ=256 chars but otherwise just consumes data. `_open=-1`, `_exit` does ebreak in inf loop, `_kill`/`_getpid` ebreak. `_sbrk` uses `__heap_start__`/`__heap_end__`. Provides `operator new/delete` and array variants via malloc/free.\n\n### /app/toolchain/crt/coralnpu_htif_gloss.cc — Behavior\n\nHTIF semihosting. Defines `tohost`, `fromhost`, `tohost_ready`, `fromhost_ready` 64-bit volatile aligned-64 in `.htif` section. `htif_syscall_6args(n,a0..a5)` builds an 8-element packet, fences, writes its address to `tohost`, sets `tohost_ready=1`, polls until `fromhost!=0 || fromhost_ready!=0`, fences, returns `buf[0]`. Implements weak `_open` (translates newlib flags to Linux openat flags), `_read`, `_write`, `_close`, `_lseek`, `_fstat` (returns S_IFREG), `_isatty` (1 for fd 0..2), `_exit` (writes `(status<<1)|1` to tohost then `wfi` loop), `_kill`, `_getpid`, `_sbrk`. Syscall numbers: openat=56, close=57, lseek=62, read=63, write=64, fstat=80, exit=93, getmainvars=2011; AT_FDCWD=-100.\n\n### /app/toolchain/crt/coralnpu_exceptions.cc — FULL\n\nProvides weak `coralnpu_exception_handler()` that does `ebreak` then infinite loop.\n\n### /app/toolchain/crt/cxx_guards.cc — Behavior\n\nImplements `__cxxabiv1::__cxa_guard_acquire/release/abort` (thread-unsafe single-threaded). `__cxa_atexit` stores up to MAX_ATEXIT_ENTRIES=8 destructors. `__cxa_finalize` runs them in reverse, then resets `atexit_count=0`. `__cxa_pure_virtual` ebreaks.\n\n---\n\n## 14. /app/tests/cocotb/build_defs.bzl — FULL CONTENT\n\nDefines:\n- `VERILATOR_BUILD_ARGS` — list including `-Wno-WIDTH`, `-Wno-CASEINCOMPLETE`, `-Wno-LATCH`, `-Wno-SIDEEFFECT`, `-Wno-MULTIDRIVEN`, `-Wno-UNOPTFLAT`, `-Wno-BLKANDNBLK`, `-Wno-CASEX`, `-Wno-ASCRANGE`, `-Wno-WIDTHEXPAND`, `-Wno-WIDTHTRUNC`, `-Wno-UNSIGNED`, `-DUSE_GENERIC=\"\"`, `-DTB_SUPPORT`, `-DZVE32F_ON`, `-DVLEN_128`, `-Ihdl/verilog`, `-LDFLAGS \"-rdynamic\"`.\n- `VCS_BUILD_ARGS` — `-timescale=1ns/1ps`, `-kdb`, `+vcs+fsdbon`, `-debug_access+all`, `-cm line+cond+tgl+branch+assert`, `-cm_hier ../tests/cocotb/coverage_exclude.cfg`, `+notimingcheck`, `+nospecify`, `-hsopt=ignoreasiccap`, `-LDFLAGS -rdynamic`, `-CFLAGS -I../hdl/verilog`, `../hdl/verilog/sram_backdoor.cc`.\n- `VCS_TEST_ARGS` — `+vcs+fsdbon`, `+fsdb+mda`, `+fsdb+struct`, `-cm line+cond+tgl+branch+assert`.\n- `VCS_DEFINES = {\"USE_GENERIC\":\"\", \"TB_SUPPORT\":\"\", \"ZVE32F_ON\":\"\", \"VLEN_128\":\"\"}`.\n- `rvv_core_mini_axi_netlist_test_suite(name, vcs_verilog_sources, vcs_build_args_extra=[], vcs_data_extra=[], **kwargs)` calls `cocotb_test_suite` with `simulators=[\"vcs_netlist\"]` and a fixed list of testcases:\n  - Overview & Top-Level Structure\n\nCoral NPU is a Google open-source RISC-V based ML accelerator (`rv32imf_zve32x_zicsr_zifencei_zbb`). The codebase is a Bazel-managed monorepo. **All RTL/firmware sources have been removed; only test infrastructure, BUILD files, documentation, and a couple of supporting C++ DPI files remain.**\n\n### Top-Level Directories (`/app/`)\n| Path | Purpose |\n| --- | --- |\n| `/app/.bazelrc`, `/app/.bazelversion`, `/app/WORKSPACE`, `/app/BUILD.bazel` | Bazel workspace config |\n| `/app/README.md`, `/app/CONTRIBUTING.md`, `/app/LICENSE` | Project metadata |\n| `/app/doc/` | Architecture & microarch documentation |\n| `/app/hdl/` | Hardware description (Chisel + Verilog) — sources removed |\n| `/app/tests/` | Verilator C++ TBs, cocotb Python+SV tests |\n| `/app/sw/` | NPU-targeted software/sim utilities |\n| `/app/fpga/` | FPGA build harness (Verilator/Nexus/IP cores) |\n| `/app/examples/` | Sample programs (`hello_world_add_floats.cc`, etc.) |\n| `/app/coralnpu_test_utils/` | Python test interface libraries |\n| `/app/lib/`, `/app/utils/`, `/app/rules/`, `/app/toolchain/`, `/app/third_party/`, `/app/platforms/` | Build glue |\n| `/app/hw_sim/`, `/app/run_isp_cam_sim.sh`, `/app/jtag-sim.cfg` | Simulator runners |\n\n## 2. Key Documentation\n\n### `/app/README.md`\n- Coral NPU = NPU/AI accelerator, RV32IMF + Zve32x.\n- **Features**: 4-stage pipeline; in-order dispatch / out-of-order retire; 4-way scalar, 2-way vector dispatch; 128-bit SIMD (256-bit future); 8 KB ITCM + 32 KB DTCM single-cycle SRAMs; AXI4 master/slave.\n- Quick-start commands: `bazel run //tests/cocotb:core_mini_axi_sim_cocotb`, build `//examples:coralnpu_v2_hello_world_add_floats`, build `//tests/verilator_sim:core_mini_axi_sim`.\n\n### `/app/doc/overview.md`\n- Three processor components (matrix, vector/SIMD, scalar) fused around a quantized outer-product MAC engine (256 MACs/cycle).\n- Custom RV32IM frontend; the C-extension encoding is reclaimed for SIMD register indices and stripmining.\n- Backwards branches taken / forwards not-taken policy in fetch.\n- 31 scalar regs, 64 vector regs (256-bit), 8x8x32 accumulator.\n- Stripmining: a single dispatch like `vadd v0` produces 4 issues `vadd v0..v3` to backend.\n- L1Icache: 8 KB, 4-way associative, 256-bit blocks. L1Dcache: 16 KB, 4-way, dual 8 KB banks; flushing stalls until completion.\n\n### `/app/doc/integration_guide.md`\n- Describes the AXI integration variant (`core_mini_axi_cc_library_emit_verilog`).\n- Module ports: `clk`, `reset`, `s_axi`, `m_axi`, `irqn`, `wfi`, `debug`, `halted`, `fault`.\n- AXI master/slave channel signal semantics.\n- Memory map: ITCM `0x0000–0x1FFF` (8 kB), DTCM `0x10000–0x17FFF` (32 kB), CSR `0x30000+`.\n- External CSRs: `RESET_CONTROL` (`0x0`, RESET[0], CLOCK_GATE[1]), `PC_START` (`0x4`), `STATUS` (`0x8`, HALTED[0], FAULT[1]).\n- Boot sequence: load ITCM, write PC_START, release clock gate, release reset, monitor STATUS.\n- Synchronous reset strategy.\n\n### `/app/doc/microarch/microarch.md`\n- 3-stage in-order pipeline (Fetch → Decode/Dispatch → Execute/Writeback).\n- Dispatches up to 4 instructions/cycle with scoreboard/interlock.\n- Latencies: Alu/Csr/Bru = 1, Mlu = 2, Dvu = variable, Lsu = 2+.\n\n### `/app/doc/microarch/dispatch.md`\n- In-order; if instr at addr n cannot dispatch, n+4 isn't either.\n- Scoreboarding for RAW/WAW; WAR avoided via day-after read.\n- Single Mlu, non-pipelined Dvu.\n- Conservative jump barrier: `jal/jalr/ebreak/ecall/mret/wfi`.\n- `csrrw/csrrs/csrrc/ebreak/ecall/mret/fence/fenci/wfi` are slot-0 only.\n\n### `/app/doc/microarch/lsu.md`\n- Per-slot table managing single LSU operation; states Idle → VectorUpdate → TransferMemory → Writeback.\n- Three buses: `ibus` (read-only 128-bit), `dbus` (TCM, 128-bit + 16-bit wmask), `ebus` (external, has fault sub-bundle: valid/write/addr/epc).\n- `rvv2lsu`/`lsu2rvv` interfaces wired to vector regfile (idx + vregfile + mask, 128-bit data).\n\n### `/app/doc/microarch/mlu.md`\n- 4 per-lane req inputs (op, addr, valid, ready, rs1/rs2 valid+data).\n- Single rd output (valid, addr 5-bit, data 32-bit, ready).\n- 3 stages: dispatch → compute → writeback.\n\n### `/app/doc/peripherals/dma.md`\n- DMA engine spec: dual TLUL ports (host 128-bit, device 32-bit). Located in `hdl/chisel/src/bus/DmaEngine.scala`.\n- Base 0x40050000. Registers: CTRL/STATUS/DESC_ADDR/CUR_DESC/XFER_REMAIN.\n- 32-byte descriptor format; FSM IDLE→FETCH_DESC_0/1→POLL_CHECK→XFER_READ/WRITE→DONE.\n- Modes: mem→mem, mem→peripheral, peripheral→mem; up to 16 MB transfer.\n\nOther docs: `/app/doc/microarch/debug.md`, `/app/doc/sw/dma.md`, `/app/doc/simulation.md`, `/app/doc/tutorials/{writing_coralnpu_programs,npusim_mobilenet_tutorial}.md`.\n\n## 3. Existing Source Files (Files NOT Deleted)\n\nThe only HDL source files still present are:\n\n| Path | Purpose |\n| --- | --- |\n| `/app/hdl/verilog/sram_backdoor.cc` | DPI-C SRAM backdoor for simulation; functions `sram_init`, `sram_read`, `sram_write`, `sram_cleanup`, `sram_backdoor_load_c`, `sram_load_elf`. Tracks SRAMs by global address, supports late-bound loads (data may arrive before SRAM registers itself). Parses 32-bit ELFs and copies PT_LOAD segments via `SramBackdoorLoad`. |\n| `/app/hdl/verilog/sram_backdoor.h` | Public headers including `coralnpu::SramBackdoorLoad` and C-ABI `sram_backdoor_load_c`. |\n| `/app/hdl/verilog/sram_backdoor.core` | FuseSoC core file. |\n\nOther (referenced but missing) Verilog files exported by `/app/hdl/verilog/BUILD`:\n- `ClockGate.sv`, `RstSync.sv` (used by Chisel `:clock_gate` / `:RstSync` libs as resources)\n- `Sram.v`, `Sram_1rw_256x256.v`, `Sram_1rwm_256x288.v` (used by `:srams`, l1icache/l1dcache)\n- `TlulAdapterSram.sv`, `Uart.sv`\n\nThe `/app/hdl/verilog/rvv/{common,design,inc}/BUILD` files reference dozens of `rvv_backend_*.sv`/.svh modules + FPnew/C910_DivSqrt verilog (missing) and an `Aligner_tb.sv` / `MultiFifo_tb.sv`.\n\nThe Chisel test specs (file count: 31) all live under `/app/hdl/chisel/src/`. zero\n        mv   a2, zero\n        mv   a3, zero\n        mv   a4, zero\n        mv   a5, zero\n        mv   a6, zero\n        mv   a7, zero\n        mv   s2, zero\n        mv   s3, zero\n        mv        # Run constructors\n        la   s0, __init_array_start__\n        la   s1, __init_array_end__\n        bgeu s0, s1, init_array_loop_end\ninit_array_loop:\n        lw   t0, 0(s0)\n        jalr t0\n        addi s0, s0, 0x4\n        bltu s0, s1, init_array_loop\ninit_array_loop_end:\n\n        # Set up a default trap vector, which will\n        # simply call ebreak.\n        # Users who require real trap handling should\n        # install their own trap vector.\n        la t0, coralnpu_exception_handler\n        csrw mtvec, t0\n\n        # Set FS (0x6000) and VS (0x600) to Dirty\n        li      t0, 0x6600\n        csrrs   zero, mstatus, t0\n\n        # Set up sentinel value in _ret\n        # If we see this after an ebreak,\n        # the break source is unlikely to\n        # be a clean return from main.\n        la   t0, _ret\n        li   a0, 0x0badd00d\n        sw   a0, 0(t0)\n\n        #############\n        # Call main #\n        #############\n        li   a0, 0  # argv\n        li   a1, 0  # argc\n        la   ra, main\n        jalr ra, ra\n        # Save return value\n        mv   s2, a0\n\n        # Call C++ finalizers\n        call __cxa_finalize\n\n        # Initialize finalization arrays (destructors)\n        # These are called in reverse order.\n        la   s0, __fini_array_start__\n        la   s1, __fini_array_end__\n        beq  s0, s1, fini_array_loop_end\nfini_array_loop:\n        addi s1, s1, -0x4\n        lw   t0, 0(s1)\n        jalr t0\n        bne  s0, s1, fini_array_loop\nfini_array_loop_end:\n\n        # Restore the application's return value\n        mv   a0, s2\n        la   t0, _ret\n        sw   a0, 0(t0)\n        beqz a0, success\nfailure:\n        ebreak\n        j    loop\nsuccess:\n        # Commit instruction retirement counters to architectural state.\n        # This is for co-simulation verification purposes.\n        csrr a0, minstret\n        csrr a1, minstreth\n        .word 0x08000073 # mpause\nloop:\n        j    loop\n```\n\n---\n\n## Notes on the CoreMiniAxi.el File\n\nThat file (1155 lines) is a Cadence Verisium IES coverage exclusion file (`Format Version: 2`).
